# Bacte
Aliases: B, Ba, Bac, Bact
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Mob pages missing internal IDs, Rift Dimension
[Bacte]
Type: [[Mob]]
Spawn Location: Colosseum ([[Colosseum (Rift)]])
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000
[Drops]
Drops: 8x [[Bacte Fragment]] 0–1x [[Celadon Dye]]
Bacte is the second boss in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It requires the [[Living Timecharm]]. It can be fought in the [[Colosseum (Rift)|Colosseum]].
## Spawning
Bacte starts out as a Slime named B in the center of the [[Colosseum (Rift)|Colosseum]]. The player can start the boss fight by punching all of the [[Globowl|Globowls]] in the arena into B.
B and the Globowls will respawn after 3 minutes and 30 seconds counting from boss disappearing from arena.
## Drops
When Bacte is killed, every player fighting it will receive:
Item drops - [Lv?]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Bacte Fragment]]|8|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Celadon Dye]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.01%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv?]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Motes]]|10,000|10,000|10,000|
## Behavior
While in the boss fight, the player will die if they go outside the arena for more than about 3 seconds. Bacte will despawn if no players are in the arena.
Bacte is invulnerable for most of the boss fight. There are 5 phases of the fight, which start when Bacte has 1,000 [[Health]], 800 [[Health]], 600 [[Health]], 400 [[Health]], and 200 [[Health]]. At the start of each phase after the first, the next letter of Bacte's name will be revealed. During each phase, Bacte will root an increasing amount of tentacles into the ground, which can only be damaged by Globowls. They have 4 in the first 3 phases, and 3 in phase 4. After a tentacle is destroyed, a [[Blobbercyst]] will spawn at its location. After each tentacle and Blobbercyst is destroyed, Bacte will shrink down, and can be damaged by players, until the next phase starts or Bacte is killed.
During the phases, Bacte will use the following attacks:
- Spikes Pandemoglob (Spike) - Creates spikes of slime that rise from the floor of the arena, taking 20s [[Rift Time]] from players they come in contact with and knocking them high into the air.
- Malign Sliceline (Slice) - Creates a border in a half or quarter of the arena (the Safe Slice), and starts a countdown of a few seconds. If the player is not within the area when the countdown ends, they will lose 30s [[Rift Time]].
- Slimecopter - Creates a line of slime that spins around Bacte. It takes 10s [[Rift Time]] from players it comes in contact with and knocks them into the air.
Bacte will use more attacks the further into the boss fight the player is.
- Phase 1: B will not use any attacks, and spawn 1 tentacle.
- Phase 2: Ba will cast Spikes Pandemoglob, before spawning 2 tentacles.
- Phase 3: Bac will cast Malign Sliceline, then Spikes Pandemoglob, before spawning 3 tentacles.
- Phase 4: Bact will cast Slimecopter, which will reverse 4 times and speed up 3 times, before slowing down. It will then cast Spikes Pandemoglob and Malign Sliceline, before summoning 4 tentacles, which all have 3 [[Health]] .
- Phase 5: Bacte will cast an unnamed spell, which will turn the arena into Slime Blocks, and the player will begin to bounce very high. They can left click to use their Blaster, which deals one damage to tentacles. Bacte will use Spikes Pandemoglob, Malign Sliceline, and Slimecopter, before spawning 6 tentacles, with each having 20 [[Health]] . Blobbercysts will not spawn during this phase, but Globowls sometimes can due to a bug.
Once the tentacles are rooted into the ground, the frequency of Malign Sliceline attacks is reduced, and players have 8 seconds instead of 5 seconds to get to the safe slice.
## Tips
- It is highly recommended to gather a few people beforehand, as it greatly speeds up the battle.
- Blobbercysts take 100 damage if a Globowl is pushed towards them. If done consistently, this is quicker than melee, and can be used to kill them without walking towards them.
- Server-hopping is a valid method of gaining fragments quicker, at the cost of bits.

## Trivia
- Bacte is a clear reference to [[Dante]].
- According to [[Elise (Rift)|Elise]], either Bacte or Dante may have originated from the other. Additionally, Shen may have found Dante in the Rift.
- Using the command /killbacte, a reference to [[Commands|/killdante]], will make the following message appear in chat: ----------------------------------------------------- BACTE HAS NOT BEEN DEFEATED This command has been ran [#] times! Hopefully those players felt bad [#] times about it. >:[ -----------------------------------------------------
  - Occasionally, it may show the following message instead if the kill count hasn't been loaded yet: ----------------------------------------------------- BACTE HAS NOT BEEN DEFEATED This command has been ran many times! Hopefully those players felt bad about it. >:[ -----------------------------------------------------
- Everyone who qualified for the fragment reward rolls the chance for the dye, no matter the damage or boss stage of entering the arena.
- Completing the fight rewards 6,000 [[Motes]], and killing the Bacte mob rewards 4,000 [[Motes]], even through lootshare for a total of 10,000 [[Motes]].

# Bal
Aliases: Baal
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, More info needed, Mobs with Bestiary data, Confirmations needed, Crystal Hollows, Bosses
[Bal]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: BOSS ([[Bosses]])
Mob Level: 263
Spawn Location: [[Khazad-dûm]]
Mob Type: Cubic ([[Cubic (Mob Type)]]) Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]]) [[Shielded]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 200; [[Damage]]: 100
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Yoggie]] 0–1x LEGENDARY Bal Pet ([[Bal Pet]]) 0–1x EPIC Bal Pet ([[Bal Pet]]) 0–1x Flawed Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 0–1x Flawed Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])
XP: +1,972 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 789
[Metadata]
ID: BAL_BOSS_100
Bal is a [[Mob]] and [[Bosses|BOSS]] that spawns every minute in [[Khazad-dûm]] if a player is nearby. Killing the boss allows the player to obtain the [[Gemstone Crystals|Topaz Crystal]].
## Spawning
Bal spawns every minute while a player is within [[Khazad-dûm]], which can be found randomly generated in the lower Y levels of the [[Crystal Hollows]]. Bal will appear in the center as an expanding Magma Cube and the [[Gemstone Crystals|Topaz Crystal]] will disappear.
## Behavior
Bal will hop towards the closest player, summoning [[Fire Bat|Fire Bats]] and [[Bald Blaze|Bald Blazes]] to attack the player when hit, and shooting fireballs at an interval of once every hop. In addition, it will deal contact damage that pushes the player away like a normal [[Magma Cube]], although the knockback can be avoided with a [[Knockback]]-resistant item. These attacks deal low damage, with the Fire Bats and Bald Blazes ignoring the player if they have an [[Intimidation Artifact]] or a [[Parrot Pet]].
When an item is dropped, there is a chance that Bal will summon [[Fire Bat|Fire Bats]] or [[Bald Blaze|Bald Blazes]].
Upon being defeated, it will teleport to the middle and slowly shrink, before disappearing. Afterwards, the [[Gemstone Crystals|Topaz Crystal]] will appear nearby. It can be claimed by any player who damaged the boss until it respawns.
When alive, it makes loud slowed angry enderman sounds, which can be heard from a medium distance away.
## Drops
Upon every hit, Bal has a chance to drop items on the ground similar to dungeon blessings. These items will automatically collect themselves in around three minutes. Over the duration of a Bal fight, around 25-40 items will drop. These items can be:

|-|-|-|-|
|Flawed Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|5%|
|Flawed Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|5%|
|[[Yoggie]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|4.5%|
|EPIC Bal Pet ([[Bal Pet]])|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.0126%|
|LEGENDARY Bal Pet ([[Bal Pet]])|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.0054%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv263]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|

|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|1,972|1,972|1,972|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|789|789|789|
Bal can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] to obtain [[Bal Shard]] and +5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. [[Charm Chance]] has no effect on it, and instead is 0.125% (1 in 800)[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; Check if salt is needed; When this info is added, remove this.) per hit.

## Trivia
- Bal's health is displayed as ??? [[Health]] .
- Bal is based on the Balrog from J.R.R. Tolkien's Lord of the Rings series.
  - Its spawning place, [[Khazad-dûm]], is also based on the place of the same name.
- It used to be possible to trap Bal by building a cage around its spawn point.
  - Building in [[Khazad-dûm]] is now limited in order to prevent this.
- Bal has fixed invulnerability frames, meaning it will only take damage approximately once every 0.3s[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], even if the player has 100 [[Attack Speed]], uses [[Ferocity]] or multiple arrows.
  - Certain items such as the [[Souls Rebound]] (currently disabled) or the [[Wither Cloak Sword]] allow players to bypass this.
- Bal can be [[Corrupted Mobs|corrupted]] via [[Totem of Corruption]], however no [[Sulphur]] or [[Corrupted Fragment|Corrupted Fragments]] drop.
  - [[Totem of Corruption]] has since be changed to only corrupt sea creatures, making it impossible to corrupt Bal.
- Bal cannot be damaged by the [[Frozen Scythe]] or [[Glacial Scythe]].
- The name Bal or Baal originates from proto-Semitic word for "owner" or "master". It got into English through Phoenician, Hebrew, Greek and Latin, where on top of the previously mentioned it gained the meaning "Lord". It is cognate with Beelzebub and Belphegor, two names for the Devil.
## Bugs
- Bal will occasionally fail to respawn. This can be fixed by having all players leave [[Khazad-dûm]] for a moment.

# Bald Blaze
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Crystal Hollows
[Bald Blaze]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 16
Spawn Location: [[Khazad-dûm]]
Mob Type: Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 600; [[Damage]]: 150
[Drops]
Drops: 2x [[Blaze Rod]]
XP: +24 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 17
[Metadata]
ID: BLAZE_15
The Bald Blaze is a [[Mob]] found in the [[Khazad-dûm]] during [[Bal]] boss fight.
## Spawning
6 Bald Blazes randomly spawn after hitting [[Bal]] in the [[Khazad-dûm]].
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv16]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Blaze Rod]]|2|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv16]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|5|5|5|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|24|24|24|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|17|17|17|
## Behavior
When idle, Blazes stay on the ground and slowly wander around. When they sense the player, they fly upwards, and try to remain 0.5-3.5 blocks above their target. Blazes attack players by shooting a volley of 6 fireballs, usually when the player is within 16 blocks, but may sometimes start attacking from as far as 24 blocks. They shoot 5 fireballs in a quick succession, 0.2 seconds between each shot. Afterwards, they stop attacking for about 2 seconds.

The Barbarian is a [[Mob]] that spawn in the [[Barbarian Outpost]]. They wield a sword which they use to attack the player.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv211]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Cyclamen Dye]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.00004%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv211]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|70|70|70|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|316|316|316|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|232|232|232|
Killing a Barbarian will make the player gain [[Factions|5 ቾ Mage Reputation]] and lose [[Factions|5 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]], up to a limit of [[Factions|0 ቾ Mage Reputation]] and [[Factions|0 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]].
## Behavior
Barbarians wield an Iron Sword (with the metadata of the [[Raider Sword]], as shown through texture packs) which they use to attack the player, only if they were provoked first or the player has less than [[Factions|-1000 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]]. They deal 3,500 [[Damage]] on hit.

# Barbarian Duke X
Aliases: Barb Duke, Barb duke, Barbarian Duke, Duke X, Duke x
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Crimson Isle, Minibosses
[Barbarian Duke X]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])
Mob Level: 486
Spawn Location: [[The Dukedom]]
Special Behavior: Jumps up in the air and shoots lasers
Mob Type: [[Humanoid]] [[Animal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 70,000,000; [[Damage]]: 2,500
[Drops]
Drops: 20–35x [[Raw Porkchop]] 25x [[Crimson Essence]] 0–2x [[Enchanted Raw Porkchop]] 1x [[Leather Cloth]] 0–1x Hot Kuudra Key ([[Kuudra Keys]]) 0–1x [[Magma Urchin]] 0–1x [[Cyclamen Dye]] 0–1x Kuudra Key ([[Kuudra Keys]]) 0–1x [[Flaming Fist]]
XP: +3,645 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1458
[Metadata]
ID: BARBARIAN_DUKE_X_200
The Barbarian Duke X is a hostile [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] that can be found in [[The Dukedom]] which is located on North side of [[Burning Desert]] of [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
The Barbarian Duke X spawns in [[The Dukedom]] in the Barbarian Faction side of the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Behavior
The Barbarian Duke X can only be hit inside the Flame Domain, a circle of particles around part of [[The Dukedom]]. It normally runs towards nearby players, dealing 4,500 [[Damage]] on hit.
It also has multiple abilities:
- Leap - It will occasionally create a circle of purple particles in an area of the arena and jump into the air, landing in the circle. Any players in the circle when it lands will take [[Damage]], have their FOVs zoomed in, and will be unable to move for a few seconds afterward. The frequency and speed of these attacks increase as the Barbarian Duke X's [[Health]] lowers.
  - If the boss goes out of the Flame Domain, the radius of its attack will be increased to almost the entire arena.
- Flaming Fist - Shoots many Flaming Fists outward when a player is in close range. They deal [[Damage]] on hit.
## Drops
Item drops are rolled for all players that dealt 250,000 [[Damage]] or more to the Barbarian Duke X:

[Metadata]
ID: BARBARIANS_GUARD_200
Barbarian Guard is an [[Mob]] that patrols [[Dragontail]].
## Spawning
Barbarian Guards can be found patrolling [[Dragontail]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Behavior
If a player attacks the guard at least three times, the player will be attacked. When hostile, the Barbarian Guard has 50,000,000 [[Health]] and deals 20,000 [[Damage]].
Once a player has been targeted, the guards will chase all the way even into the upstairs of a building. [[Slowness Potion|Slowness]] splash potions can drastically obstruct their movement, useful when the player has difficulty on accessing merchant NPCs.

A Belle is a [[Mob]] that spawns as a projectile from [[Executive Sebastian]], a [[Team Treasurite]] member found in the [[Mithril Deposits]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Spawning
Belles can be spawned when fighting [[Executive Sebastian]]. After a few seconds, it will despawn, before another one gets spawned again.
## Behavior
Belles will run towards the player it was shot towards to attack them. It despawns after a few seconds.

## Behavior
The Bezal floats in the air and fires multiple fireballs at the player every few seconds. It will not fly if a [[Moody Grappleshot]] has been used on it. Upon being killed, it deals 1000 [[Damage]] to the player, and sets them on fire. The message "The nearby blaze died and set you on fire for 1000 damage!" will be displayed in chat.

# Bladesoul
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Crimson Isle

[Drops]

[[Experience]] Orbs: 1431
[Metadata]
ID: BLADESOUL_200
Bladesoul is a [[Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] that spawns in the [[Stronghold]] area in a small, circular arena to the right of the [[Crimson Isle]] entrance.
## Spawning
Bladesoul spawns around 5 minutes after being defeated at [[Coordinates|-295, 83, -518]].
## Behavior
- Bladesoul will attack one player at a time using its [[Ragnarock]], dealing 3,000 [[Damage]] on hit.
- Bladesoul will sometimes shoot wither skulls at all nearby players, dealing 7,500 [[Damage]] on hit.
- Occasionally, Bladesoul will shoot a ring of wither skulls directly outward, instantly killing any player they hit (can't be nullified by abilities, such as the esoward invulnerable). Before the attack, a circle of particles appears around Bladesoul.
- Bladesoul will sometimes turn a nearby block into a Block of Coal, which deals [[Damage]] to players who stand on top of it. After 10 seconds, a Wither Guard will be spawned, and Bladesoul's [[Health]] will be increased by 12,500,000. While the Wither Guard is alive, it increases Bladesoul's maximum [[Health]] by 12,500,000. After the Wither Guard has died, Bladesoul loses 12,500,000 [[Health]] and maximum [[Health]].
  - The Wither Guard has 5,000,000 [[Health]] and deals 4,000 [[Damage]]. It quickly runs towards nearby players.
  - If Bladesoul's [[Health]] is over 100,000,000, he will become invincible until his maximum [[Health]] is below 100,000,000.
## Drops
Item drops are rolled for all players that deal 250,000 [[Damage]] or more to Bladesoul.

The three Blazes are a group of [[Mob|Mobs]] found past the spawn of [[Crimson Isle]] and in the [[Stronghold]], several blocks away from [[Elle]]. When killed, they never spawn again, and her quest begins.
## Spawning
The three Blazes can be found just past the spawn of the [[Crimson Isle]], in the [[Stronghold]]'s entrance next to the right of [[Elle]].
## Behavior
The three Blazes stay still and occasionally shoot fireballs towards the player and [[Elle]]. Once killed, they will never respawn again, and the player can speak to [[Elle]] to continue her quest, leading to the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]].

# Blaze (Higher or Lower)
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Blaze]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 11-27
Spawn Location: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Mob Type: Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000-6,499
[Drops]
XP: 16-40 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 12-29
[Metadata]
ID: BLAZE_HIGHER_OR_LOWER_15; MASTER_BLAZE_HIGHER_OR_LOWER_15
Blazes are [[Mob|Mobs]] found in the Higher or Lower [[Dungeon Puzzle Rooms|Dungeon Puzzle Room]]. They are passive and spawn with 1,000-6,499 [[Health]].
## Spawning
Ten Blazes spawn in the Higher or Lower [[Dungeon Puzzle Rooms|Dungeon Puzzle Room]] with varying [[Health]] values.
The first blaze that spawns has a [[Health]] value between 1,000-1,999 , based on the following formula:
Health=rand(0, 1000)+1000
Each blaze after that has the [[Health]] of the previous blaze, with an extra 1-500 [[Health]], based on the following formula:
Health=Previous Health+rand(0, 500)+1
This means that the theoretical maximum [[Health]] that the last blaze could have is 6,499 [[Health]], though the highest value out of all seeds of Java's Random#nextInt is 6,420 , at 19,791,523,121,448.
## Behavior
The ten Blazes in the room do not move, nor do they attack the player. They must be killed in a specific order to complete the puzzle. If the room's entrance is at the bottom, then they must be killed from lowest to highest [[Health]]. Otherwise, they must be killed from highest to lowest [[Health]].

## Trivia

The Blazing Golem is a [[Mob]] that spawns during the [[Kuudra]] fight in [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
## Spawning
Blazing Golems occasionally spawn in batches during later waves in the [[Kuudra]] fight. They can spawn starting from the 16th wave.
## Behavior
Blazing Golems frequently shoot exploding iron blocks towards players, dealing 0.6x the base [[Damage]]. They also occasionally jumps towards players, with its landing spot indicated by a ring of particles, dealing 1.5x the base [[Damage]].

The Blight is a [[Mob]] that spawns during the [[Kuudra]] fight in [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
## Spawning
Blights occasionally spawn in batches during new waves in the [[Kuudra]] fight. They can spawn starting from the third wave.
## Behavior
Blights will quickly run towards nearby players to attack them. They have three phases, each with a different armor color. They cannot be damaged during the red phase, but this can be partially mitigated with [[Kuudra|Crowd Control Blight Slayer]] perk.

# Bonzo
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Pages with UIs, Catacombs, Dungeons, Bosses, Boss Collections
Gratz for making it this far, but I'm basically unbeatable. I don't even need to fight, this is the life! I can summon lots of Undead. Check this out!
— Bonzo
[Bonzo]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: BOSS ([[Bosses]])
Mob Level: 221-756
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor I]]
Description: New Necromancer; Involved in the dark arts due to his parents' insistence. Originally worked as a Circus Clown.
Mob Type: [[Humanoid]] (Phase 1) Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]]) (Phase 2) [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 250,000-200,000,000; [[Damage]]: 2,664-45,000
[Drops]
XP: 1,657-5,670 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 663-2268
[Metadata]
ID: BONZO_1; MASTER_BONZO_1
Bonzo is the [[Bosses|BOSS]] of [[The Catacombs - Floor I|Catacombs - Floor I]] of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] Dungeon.
## Collection
Killing Bonzo levels up the Bonzo Collection.
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|25|[[Red Nose]] Reward|+15|
|II|50|[[Bonzo's Mask]] Reward|+15|
|III|100|Golden Bonzo Head ([[Golden Heads]]) Reward; Gold ([[Gold Essence]]) Essence|+15|
|IV|150|[[Bonzo's Staff]] Reward|+25|
|V|250|[[Recombobulator 3000]] Reward|+25|
|VI|1,000|Diamond Bonzo Head ([[Diamond Heads]]) Recipe; Diamond ([[Diamond Essence]]) Essence|+25|
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## The Fight
### First Phase
Bonzo will continually summon [[Undead (Bonzo)|Undeads]], and run around attacking players with wither skulls during his first phase. Bonzo holds the [[Dark Orb]] during the first phase. Bonzo has 250,000 [[Health]] and will move on to the second phase once players have killed his first phase.
### Second Phase
Bonzo revives during his second phase, healing himself back to his max health and gaining Default Attack and Balloon Barrage.
### Arena
Bonzo's arena is open in the center but has 7 different short pillars close to the edges. It heavily resembles a Circus arena, and Bonzo's first phase can go on top of the pillars. In his second phase, Bonzo's Balloon Barrage attack can aim his balloons on top of the pillars, meaning the only way to defend yourself against the attack is to either dodge the balloons yourself or hide behind a pillar.
## Attacks
- Default Attack - Strikes the player in melee range for damage.
  - Bonzo uses this attack only during the second phase.
- Wither Skull - Bonzo fires a Wither Skull at a player dealing 1,598 [[Damage]].
  - Bonzo uses this attack only during the first phase.
  - In Master Mode, Bonzo shoots Wither Skulls more quickly.
- Bonzo's Balloon - Bonzo fires a balloon at a player, dealing 1,598 [[Damage]] in normal mode and 22,500 [[Damage]] in [[Catacombs|Master Mode]].
  - Bonzo uses this attack only during the second phase.
  - This move is identical to Wither Skull.
  - In Master Mode, he tends to shoot his balloons at an upward angle.
- Balloon Barrage - Bonzo moves to the center of the arena before unleashing a barrage of balloons around him in all directions.
  - Bonzo uses this attack only during the second phase.
- Summon Undead- Bonzo will summon some [[Undead (Bonzo)|Undeads]] to attack the player. The Undeads only have 45,000 [[Health]] and act like regular Zombies.
  - Bonzo uses this attack only during the first phase.
## Master Mode Changes
- Bonzo has 200M [[Health]] and his Undeads have 15M [[Health]] .
- Bonzo spawns Undeads more frequently in his 1st phase.
- The Wither Skull and Balloon attacks deal 22,500 [[Damage]].
## Drops
Killing Bonzo in both phase one and two directly grants the following drops:
Non-item drops - [Lv221-756]:
|Drop|Count|
|-|-|
|[[Coins]]|552-1,890|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|1,657-5,670|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|663-2,268|
Defeating him in phase two will also complete the fight, unlocking [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] with their own loot:

## Trivia
- Bonzo's second phase's design is based on the main antagonist Pennywise, the Dancing Clown from It.
  - Bonzo's first phase's skin was a skin the admin Likaos had.
- If every player in the dungeon has already completed Floor I before, or in Master Floor 1, he will just say "Oh I'm dead! Sike." when transitioning to phase two.
- The music playing in the background of the boss fight is Superior Judgement from the Hypixel Noteblock Song Competition 2020.
- Bonzo can reappear as a spirit [[The Watcher]] summons on the 3rd, 4th, and 5th Floor.
- Bonzo's line "You fools! You fell victim to one of the classic blunders!" is a reference to Vizzini from The Princess Bride.
- The item Bonzo holds in his first phase appears to be a [[Dark Orb]].
- Bonzo gets the most references by characters in the Catacombs. Every boss up to Floor 4, excluding [[The Professor]], has at least 1 thing to do with Bonzo.
  - [[The Watcher]] brings him back as an Undead.
  - [[Scarf]] has a line that says "I can't believe Bonzo lost to someone so weak!"
  - Some members of [[Thorn|Thorn's]] audience will say "Avenge Bonzo! #RememberBonzo" or "Remember Bonzo! #NeverForget"
- Bonzo had a optifine cape displaying the non-binary flag design.
- When Bonzo is summoned as an Undead is defeated, it doesn't add to the collection.

# Boss Corleone
Aliases: Corleon, Corleone
Categories: Mobs, Mob pages missing internal IDs, Confirmations needed, Mobs with Bestiary data, Crystal Hollows
[Boss Corleone]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 195
Spawn Location: [[Mithril Deposits]]
Special Behavior: Summons Smog
Mob Type: [[Humanoid]] [[Subterranean]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000,000; [[Damage]]: 4,000
[Drops]
Drops: 18–41x Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 15–27x Rough Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 0–1x [[Corleonite]]
XP: +292 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 214
Boss Corleone is a rare [[Mob]] found within two specific rooms in the [[Mithril Deposits]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]] and a member of [[Team Treasurite]]. It throws a ball that spawns a [[Smog]], and has a chance to drop [[Corleonite]].
## Spawning
Boss Corleone has a 0.76% (1 in 131) chance to spawn instead of other [[Team Treasurite]] members, but he will always spawn repeatedly in one of two special structures within the [[Mithril Deposits]]. Such rare structures can sometimes merge with other areas, such as the [[Precursor Remnants]], if they are built on the edge of the Deposits. Corleone seemingly spawns every 60 or 120 seconds alternately in these structures.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
When a player enters the Corleone spawn for the first time in a server, he will spawn no matter how long its been since the last spawn. In order for Corleone to spawn, the player must be moving near his spawn point when his spawn timer is off cooldown. It is also possible to trap Corleone into a box which reduces the risk of him spawning and attacking before the player is ready.
## Behavior
When encountered, Boss Corleone will throw a ball that spawns a [[Smog]], a small Wither with 10k [[Health]] that deals 1,500 [[Damage]]. Smog and Boss Corleone will attempt to approach the player and attack them. After a few seconds, Smog will explode, dealing damage if the player is near. Boss Corleone can spawn another Smog by throwing a ball once more. Corleone also has a 25% chance of dropping [[Corleonite]]. It also has a 100% loot share drop chance. Meaning when Corleone drops a Corleonite, everyone who attacked that Corleone will get a [[Corleonite]].

## Trivia
- Boss Corleone is likely based off Vito Corleone from Mario Puzo's 1969 novel, The Godfather.
The ball thrown by Team Treasurite members to summon their associated creature
- The mobs are based on Team Rocket from Pokémon.
  - The ball that each mob throws in battle resembles Dive Ball in Pokémon.
  - The Grunt looks like a Team Rocket Grunt, Executive Sebastian looks like Proton, Executive Wendy looks like Ariana, and Executive Viper is most likely Archer. Corleone, as the leader, is probably the counterpart of Giovanni. Within Pokémon, Executives are the highest ranking members of Team Rocket after Giovanni.

# Broodmother
Aliases: Brood Mother, Tarantula Broodmother
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Confirmations needed, Minibosses
[Broodmother]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])
Mob Level: 41
Spawn Location: [[Spider Mound]]
Special Behavior: Spawns Cave Spiders ([[Cave Spider (Broodmother)]])
Mob Type: [[Arthropod]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 3,000; [[Damage]]: 125
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[String]] 1x [[Spider Eye]] 1x [[Spider Talisman]] 0–1x EPIC Tarantula Pet ([[Tarantula Pet]]) 0–1x LEGENDARY Tarantula Pet ([[Tarantula Pet]]) 0–1x [[Travel Scroll to Spider's Den Top of Nest]] 0–1x [[Luxurious Spool]]
XP: +307 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 123
[Metadata]
ID: BROOD_MOTHER_SPIDER_12
The Broodmother is a hostile [[Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] that can be found in the [[Spider's Den]].
It can also spawn on [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] islands.
## Spawning
The Broodmother spawns at the very top of the [[Spider Mound]]. It will spawn exactly 10 minutes after the time the previous Broodmother is killed.
The status of the Broodmother changes to reflect how much time is left until she respawns.
- Slain: Between 2.5-2 minutes left.
- Dormant: Between 2-1.5 minutes left.
- Soon: Between 1.5-1 minutes left.
- Awakening: Between 1-0.5 minutes left.
- Imminent: Less than 30 seconds left.
- Alive: The Broodmother has spawned.
## Behavior
The Broodmother acts like a normal [[Spider]], attacking any nearby players.
The Broodmother spawns four or five [[Cave Spider (Broodmother)|Cave Spiders]] on death. This is the only place they spawn on Public [[Island (disambiguation)|Islands]].

|XIII|55|+37 [[Strength]]|+37 [[Magic Find]]|+26%|+6.5|+13 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

## Trivia
- The Broodmother is a very weak boss, often compared in general difficulty to the [[Revenant Horror]] Tier I.
- Killing the Broodmother for the first time gives the [[Achievements|Achievement]] "[[Achievements|A good spider is a dead spider]]", worth 10 Achievement points.
- Damage to the Broodmother is capped at 300 per hit for most weapons.
- The Broodmother, unlike other bosses, can appear in a [[Runecrafting|Runic]] version, which gives it 24000 [[Health]]. [Confirm (Confirmation needed; Is this still the runic health after the healing rework?; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- When waiting for the Broodmother to spawn, it is recommended to possess an [[Intimidation Artifact]] or an item that prevents knockback ( [[Slime Hat]], [[Rock Pet]], [[Attributes|Shell]] Attribute) to avoid being pushed off the cliff by [[Spider Jockey|Spider Jockeys]], [[Silverfish (disambiguation)|Silverfish]], or other [[Spider|Spiders]].
- In [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] mode, Broodmother spawns with 100000 [[Health]], but has the damage cap removed.

# Cellar Spider
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Dungeon Mobs
[Cellar Spider]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 58-394
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 10%
Spawn Condition: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Mob Type: [[Arthropod]] [[Subterranean]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 17,000-9,500,000; [[Damage]]: 352-97,200
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Spider Essence]]
XP: 87-591 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 63-433
[Metadata]
ID: CELLAR_SPIDER_#; MASTER_CELLAR_SPIDER_#
The Cellar Spider is a stronger version of the [[Lonely Spider]], and is often found in any Floor of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]], hiding above the doorways or entrances.
Its health, like any other [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]], scale on which floor they spawn in. It can also have modifiers such as Healthy, Flaming, Stormy, Speedy or Fortified. Healthy increases the mob's [[Health]]. Fortified increases the mob's [[Defense]]. Flaming makes melee attacks light the player on fire. Speedy makes the mob move much faster. Stormy makes the mob strike lightning on nearby players, dealing true damage.
## Spawning
Cellar Spiders spawn in certain normal rooms on all floors of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. They spawn in groups of 3–5.
## Behavior
Cellar Spiders don't attack unless the player walks under the area they are in.

# Chaosmite
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Kuudra
[Chaosmite]

The Chaosmite is a [[Mob]] that spawns during the [[Kuudra]] fight in [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
## Spawning
Chaosmites occasionally spawn during later waves in the [[Kuudra]] fight. They can spawn starting from the 35th wave.
## Behavior
Chaosmites run towards players, dealing significant [[Damage]]. Players killed from them can only be revived from another player using the Support route and spending [[Tokens]].

# Cheeky Tiki
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Article stubs, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Cheeky Tiki]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 71
Spawn Location: [[Torrhus Canyon]] [[Torrhus Heights]]
Mob Type: [[Construct]] [[Woodland]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 50,000; [[Damage]]: 300
[Drops]
Drops: 16–32x [[Helix Log]] 0–1x [[Enchanted Helix Log]]
XP: +106 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 78
[Metadata]
ID: CHEEKY_TIKI_71
A Cheeky Tiki is a [[Mob]] that can be found in the [[Torrhus Canyon]] and [[Torrhus Heights]].
## Spawning
Cheeky Tikis spawn as a totem of heads in the [[Torrhus Canyon]] and [[Torrhus Heights]]. When left-clicked, the clicked head and the one above it (or the bottom one, if the top one is clicked), will rotate clockwise from the head-down point of view, and the opposite direction when right-clicked. If the top 2 heads are facing the same direction, they will stop rotating. When all three are facing the same direction, the Cheeky Tiki awakens and attacks the player.

- [[Coordinates|-587, 152, 239]]

- [[Coordinates|-700, 132, 288]]
- [[Coordinates|-714, 135, 290]]
- [[Coordinates|-742, 136, 231]]
- [[Coordinates|-637, 142, 161]]
- [[Coordinates|-731, 130, 172]]
- [[Coordinates|-759, 125, 245]]
- [[Coordinates|-560, 144, 191]]
- [[Coordinates|-544, 139, 208]]
- [[Coordinates|-545, 131, 213]]
- [[Coordinates|-603, 172, 231]]
## Behavior
When the player awakens a Cheeky Tiki, it chases after them while blowing smoke from the top head.

# Chickzilla
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Crimson Isle
[Chickzilla]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 375
Spawn Location: [[Igrupan's Chicken Coop]]
Mob Type: [[Animal]] [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 50,000,000; [[Damage]]: 12,000
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Sulphur]]
XP: +562 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 412
[Metadata]
ID: CHICKZILLA_100
Chickzilla is a [[Mob]] spawned by [[Igrupan]] after reaching [[Factions|7,000 ቾ Mage Reputation]].
## Spawning
If the player is in the Mage [[Factions|Faction]], has completed [[Igrupan]]'s Quest, and has at least [[Factions|7,000 ቾ Mage Reputation]], Igrupan will call the player, and ask them to come to [[Igrupan's Chicken Coop]]. If the player then interacts with Igrupan, they will spawn Chickzilla.
If the player gets killed by Chickzilla, it will despawn. The player has to start over by talking to Igrupan again.
The Chickzilla fight can only be completed once per [[Profile]].
## Behavior
Chickzilla can only be damaged by melee attacks.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv375]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sulphur]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv375]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|125|125|125|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|562|562|562|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|412|412|412|
## Tips
- Since Chickzilla is an [[Animal]] mob, [[Mastiff Armor]] and a [[Pooch Sword]] is good for increased damage and damage reduction against it.
- It is recommended to have a [[Summoning Ring]] when fighting Chickzilla, as there will be a 1% chance of obtaining a [[Necromancy|Soul]] worth upwards of billions.

# Cinderbat
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Huntable Creatures, Crimson Isle
[Cinderbat]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 264
Spawn Location: [[Crimson Isle]]
Mob Type: [[Animal]] Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]]) [[Airborne]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 5,000,000; [[Damage]]: 7,000
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Flames]] 0–1x [[Cyclamen Dye]]
XP: +396 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 290
[Metadata]
ID: CINDER_BAT_90
A Cinderbat is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
Cinderbats spawn randomly hanging from blocks on the [[Crimson Isle]], usually in the caves under [[Blazing Volcano]].
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv264]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Flames]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
|[[Cyclamen Dye]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1/250k|
[Non-item drops - [Lv264]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|88|88|88|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|396|396|396|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|290|290|290|
Cinderbat can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]], put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or caught with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] to obtain [[Cinderbat Shard]] and +1,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+10,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].
## Behavior
When attacked, Cinderbats quickly fly around. Cinderbats do not have a nametag above their heads, but are surrounded by fire particles. Despite having a damage stat, they do not attack the player.
## Tips
[[Spider's Boots]] or its upgrades can allow the player to get closer before throwing their [[Lassos|Lasso]] at a Cinderbat.

[[Experience]] Orbs: 1
[Metadata]
ID: CLOTGOYLE_5
A Clotgoyle is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Stillgore Château]] while fighting a [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]].
## Behavior
3 Clotgoyles spawn while a [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]] is using its Stonewrath ability: they will walk towards the player and try to attack them, dealing 10 [[Rift Time]] and 1 [[Hearts]] per hit. Killing a Clotgoyle turns it into a cadaver, which can be destroyed during Stonewrath. All surviving cadavers are resurrected back into Clotgoyles after the phase ends.
For every Clotgoyle alive, 1 [[Hearts]] is removed from the player.
## Drops

# Crypt Ghoul
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Crypt Ghoul]
Type: [[Mob]]

XP: +49 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 36
[Metadata]
ID: UNBURRIED_ZOMBIE_30
The Crypt Ghoul is a level 30 hostile mob added in the Slayer update. It appears as a [[Zombie]] wearing Chainmail Armor and holding an [[Iron Sword]].
## Spawning
|Location|Spawn Spots (The number of spots in the location where this mob can spawn in.)|Spawn Delay (The time, after which a new mob spawns in every spot.)|Max per spot (The maximum number of mobs that can exist in each spot at a time.)|
|-|-|-|-|
|Hub Crypts ([[Crypts]])|15|14s|2|
## Drops

|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Rotten Flesh]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Rotten Flesh]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|EPIC Ghoul Pet ([[Ghoul Pet]])|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.003%|
|LEGENDARY Ghoul Pet ([[Ghoul Pet]])|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.001%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv33]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|11|11|11|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|49|49|49|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|36|36|36|
## Behavior
Crypt Ghouls attack the player using an iron sword. They have a higher movement speed than that of a regular zombie. They become hostile when the player gets within 8 blocks of them.
Sometimes Crypt Ghouls do not attack the player if they are standing right next to them. Although this is probably a bug, it is unlikely that it will get fixed.

## Tips
- Due to their high [[Combat]] experience yield, Crypt Ghouls are very good for [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayers]]. They also spawn in large quantities, making them easy to kill.
- During the [[Spooky Festival]], the maximum number of Crypt Ghouls per spot increases to 4.

The Deformed Revenant is a [[Mob]] and [[Minibosses|SLAYER MINIBOSS]] that may spawn during [[Revenant Horror]] Tier IV Quests.
## Spawning
The Deformed Revenant has a chance to spawn when a Zombie-type mob is killed during Revenant Horror Tier IV quests. Its spawning is marked by explosion sounds and particles.
## Behavior
Deformed Revenants will seek out and attack the player who summoned it. They have a short player detection range and are slower than the weaker [[Revenant Champion]].

# Diamond Goblin
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Dwarven Mines
[Diamond Goblin]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 109
Spawn Location: [[Goblin Egg]] [[Dwarven Mines]] [[Crystal Hollows]]
Mob Type: [[Timid]] [[Shielded]] [[Elusive]]

XP: +163 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 119
[Metadata]
ID: GOBLIN_500
Diamond Goblin is a passive [[Mob]] that rarely spawns instead of [[Golden Goblin|Golden Goblins]] from [[Goblin Egg|Goblin Eggs]], or by mining most [[Ores]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Spawning
Diamond Goblin has a base 3.33% chance to replace [[Golden Goblin|Golden Goblins]]. This chance can be increased by [[Gemma|Return to Sender Perk]] and [[Tracking]].
When the Diamond Goblin spawns, the following message will display in chat: A Diamond Goblin has spawned! Diamond Goblins will de-spawn after 30 seconds.
## Behavior
Diamond Goblins run away from the player, and jump off cliffs. Their behavior is similar to the Hidden Jerrys that can be spawned during [[Candidate Jerry|Mayor Jerry]]'s time in office.

## Trivia
- The Luck of the Cave perk from [[Heart of the Mountain]] increases the chance for Golden Goblins which increases the chance of Diamond Goblins to spawn.
- [[Shadow Fury]] can be used to chase a Diamond Goblin even if out of sight.
- Killing 1 Diamond Goblin is a possible task for Commission.
  - Using [[Mineral Armor]] with a [[Stonk]] in the [[Puzzler]]'s room used to be a way to complete this quest quickly. However, this was patched when the ores in the Puzzler's room were filled with planks.
- Diamond Goblins contribute to the 100 necessary kills in the Goblin Slayer commission.

# Dive Ghast

ID: DIVE_GHAST_90
The Dive Ghast is a [[Ghast]] type [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
Dive Ghasts spawn in [[The Wasteland]] at Night near the [[Forgotten Skull]]. (7 P.M. - 6 A.M. [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Time]])
## Behavior
Unlike normal Ghasts, Dive Ghasts do not shoot fireballs at the player. They make multiple igniting sounds, with particles appearing over them, before quickly flying towards the player and exploding, dealing 10,000 [[Damage]] if they hit the player. With a [[Netherrack-Looking Sunshade]], Dive Ghasts are passive.

A Dragonfly is a [[Pests|Garden Pest]]. Its corresponding crop is [[Sunflower]]. It is attracted to [[Jelly]].
## Spawning
Dragonflies can spawn in the [[The Garden]], starting at [[The Garden|Garden XI (11)]]. Dragonflies can only spawn during the day.
## Behavior
Dragonflies fly away from the player. Dragonflies can only be damaged with [[Vacuums]] or [[Lassos]].

The Dropship is a [[Mob]] that spawns during the [[Kuudra]] fight in [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
## Spawning
Dropships occasionally spawn in on wave intervals of five in the [[Kuudra]] fight.
## Behavior
Dropships fly their way to the center of the arena, after which they'll drop TNT that deals [[Damage]] to every player. When killed, it doesn't drop its TNT. It takes 1.5x [[Damage]] from cannons.

# Earthworm
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Earthworm]

An Earthworm is a [[Pests|Garden Pest]]. Its corresponding crop is [[Melon Slice]]. It is attracted to [[Compost]].
## Spawning
Earthworms can spawn in the [[The Garden]], starting at [[The Garden|Garden VI (6)]].
## Behavior
Earthworm slowly move around, burrowing to another location within the same plot after reaching 66.6% [[Health]] and 33.3% [[Health]]. This can be reduced to one time or bypassed entirely by dealing enough damage. Earthworm can only be damaged with [[Vacuums]] or [[Lassos]].

The Emerald Slime is a [[Mob]] found within in the [[Slimehill]] of the [[Deep Caverns]] and a certain room in the [[Crystal Hollows]]..
## Spawning
Emerald Slimes can primarily be found in the [[Slimehill]], located in the [[Deep Caverns]]. They also spawn within a specific structure in the [[Crystal Hollows]].

## Strategy
Players won't need to worry about Emerald Slimes multiplying, since they don't multiply upon death. Some of the larger slimes have a lot larger reach than the player, so if the player does not have much Effective [[Health]] and can get one-shot by them, they should probably fight them from afar with a bow. Also, these mobs deal knockback when they hit the player, so it is recommended that the player has [[Attributes|Shell]] attribute or use an anti-knockback item, like a [[Slime Hat]], [[Dwarf Turtle Shelmet]], or [[Rock Pet]] when killing Emerald Slimes.

# End Stone Protector
Aliases: Endstone Protector, Golem Mini Boss, Golem mini Boss
Categories: Mobs, More info needed, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Minibosses, The End, Pages with math equations, 0.7.8
[End Stone Protector]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: BOSS ([[Bosses]])
Mob Level: 310
Spawn Location: [[Dragon's Nest]]
Mob Type: [[Construct]] [[Ender]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 5,000,000; [[Defense]]: 150; [[Damage]]: 1,200
[Drops]
Drops: 1–10x [[Enchanted End Stone]] 1x [[End Stone Rose]] 0–3x [[Crystal Fragment]] 0–1x [[Golem Pet]] 0–1x [[Tier Boost Core]] 0–1x [[End Stone Protector Shard]]
XP: +2,325 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 930
[Metadata]
ID: CORRUPTED_PROTECTOR_100
The End Stone Protector is a [[Mob]] and [[Bosses|BOSS]] that spawns in several locations in the [[Dragon's Nest]] after 5,000 [[Zealot|Zealots]] are killed across the server.
## Spawning
The End Stone Protector spawns in a number of predetermined locations in the [[Dragon's Nest]]. 5,000 [[Zealot|Zealots]] must be killed in order to summon the boss.
The End Stone Protector spawns in 5 stages:
- Resting - Phase 0
- Dormant - Phase 1
- Agitated - Phase 2
- Disturbed - Phase 3
- Awakening - Phase 4
- Summoned - Phase 5
This can be seen in the player's [[Settings|Tablist Widgets]].
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|1,000 Zealots ([[Zealot]]) killed|You feel a tremor from beneath the earth!|
|5,000 kills reached|The ground begins to shake as an End Stone Protector rises from below!|
## Behavior
The End Stone Protector attacks the nearest player and rapidly swings its arms, tossing the player into the air and dealing damage. It only targets 1 player at a time.
The End Stone Protector also has 2 abilities.
- Toss: All nearby players who are on the standing on the ground are tossed into the air and take [[True Damage]].
- Crush: The End Stone Protector summons chunks of Blocks of Iron in the air and crushes players with them, dealing [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) damage.
Upon death, a platform constructed of Blocks of Iron with a Beacon in the center is generated, similar to the platforms spawned by [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]]. Players can pick up loot here.
## Drops
Non-item drops - [Lv310]:
|Drop|Count|
|-|-|
|[[Coins]]|775|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|2,325|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|930|
The drops a player receives from killing an End Stone Protector are calculated based on their loot Quality, which is calculated with this formula: Quality=BaseQuality+ZealotsKilled+50⋅PlayerDamageDealtFirstPlaceDamageDealt Up to 100 killed Zealots will contribute to the player's Quality.
Base Quality is determined by the damage placement of the player:
|Condition|Base Quality|
|-|-|
|1st in damage dealt|200|
|2nd in damage dealt|175|
|3rd in damage dealt|150|
|4th in damage dealt|125|
|5th in damage dealt|110|
|6-8th in damage dealt|100|
|9-10th in damage dealt|90|
|11-12th in damage dealt|80|
|Below 12th in damage dealt; More than 1 [[Damage]] dealt|70|
|Below 12th in damage dealt; Less than 1 [[Damage]] dealt|10|
The place the player gets guarantees them certain drops:
|Condition| [[Enchanted End Stone]]| [[Crystal Fragment]]| [[End Stone Rose]]|
|-|-|-|-|
|1st in damage dealt|10|2|1|
|2nd in damage dealt|7|1|1|
|3rd in damage dealt|4|1|1|
|4th in damage dealt|2|1|1|
|5th in damage dealt|2|1|1|
|6-8th in damage dealt|2|0|1|
|9-10th in damage dealt|1|0|1|
|11-12th in damage dealt|1|0|1|
|Below 12th in damage dealt; More than 1 [[Damage]] dealt|1|0|1|
|Below 12th in damage dealt; Less than 1 [[Damage]] dealt|1|0|1|
Certain items can be dropped from the End Stone Protector if the player has enough Quality.
If one of the unique items is dropped the player's total Quality is decreased with this formula: Quality=Quality−DropQuality
Only one player per boss can roll each unique item, meaning if multiple players get the same unique item, only one receives it.
|Order|Item|Unique|Drop Chance|Total Quality; Required|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|1|[[Tier Boost Core]]|✔|0.2%|250|
|2|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Golem Pet]]|✔|0.6%|235|
|3|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Golem Pet]]|✔|2%|220|
||[[End Stone Protector Shard]]|✕|100%|240|
||[[Pearlescent Dye]]|✕|0.001%||
[[Pearlescent Dye]] is rolled separately and does not use the weight system.
End Stone Protector can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] to obtain [[End Stone Protector Shard]] and +10,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

## Trivia
- Contributing to defeating the End Stone Protector for the first time will reward the player with the [[Achievements|"Smell like roses" Achievement]].
- The End Stone Protector previously dropped [[Flowers|Poppies]] instead of [[End Stone Rose|End Stone Roses]].
- The End Stone Protector did not drop any loot on release. This was fixed within a few hours.
- Despite the End Stone Protector being immune to knockback, mobs summoned through [[Necromancy]] are capable of dealing knockback to it.

# Enderman (Private Island)
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Ender
[Enderman]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 4-24
Spawn Location: [[Private Island]]
Mob Type: [[Ender]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 160-1132; [[Damage]]: 40-221
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Ender Pearl]]
XP: 6-36 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 4-26
[Metadata]
ID: ENDERMAN_#
The Enderman is a Neutral Mob that spawns on the [[Private Island]].
## Spawning
Endermen spawn on the player's [[Private Island]] in dark areas, although rarely. They can also be spawned by [[Enderman Minion|Enderman Minions]] on the player's island.
Endermen can also be spawned faster on a player's Private Island by using an [[Biome Stick|End Biome Stick]] This will make the island only able to spawn Endermen.
## Behavior
Endermen in SkyBlock act almost exactly like Endermen in vanilla Minecraft. They are passive when spawned, and will not attack the player, instead choosing to walk around the area they spawned into randomly. If the player attacks them or looks directly at their head, they will become aggressive, chasing the player around until they touch water, despawn, or die.
Endermen cannot be attacked using a [[Bows|Bow]] or [[Shortbows|Shortbow]] other than the [[Juju Shortbow]] or [[Terminator]].
They cannot pick up [[Dirt]] or [[Grass]] blocks on [[Private Island|Private Islands]].

## Trivia
- Wearing a [[Pumpkin]] or [[Enderman Mask]] will prevent angering Endermen by looking at them.
- Similar to in vanilla Minecraft, Endermen are immune to bows. If hit with an arrow, they won't take damage. Several bows are still able to deal damage to them, most notably the [[Juju Shortbow]] and [[Terminator]].
- When an Enderman takes damage from water, the damage number is blue.

# Exe
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Huntable Creatures, Crimson Isle
[Exe]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 6
Spawn Location: [[Mystic Marsh]]
Mob Type: [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 100; [[Damage]]: 100
[Drops]
XP: +9 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 6
[Metadata]
ID: ATOM_50
The Exe is a passive [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Mystic Marsh]].
## Behavior
Exes float in place at their spawn locations. They do not take any damage upon being hit. Using a [[Charminizer]] on an Exe will cause it to start following the player around. If 2 Exes come into contact, they will merge into a [[Wai]]. Using an [[Atominizer]] on an Exe will cause it to drop an [[X]].
## Drops
Non-item drops - [Lv6]:
|Drop|Count|
|-|-|
|[[Coins]]|2|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|9|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|6|
Using an [[Atominizer]] on an Exe will cause it to drop an [[X]].
Exe can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or caught with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] to obtain [[XYZ Shard]] and +500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

Executive Sebastian is a [[Mob]] found in the [[Mithril Deposits]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]] and a member of [[Team Treasurite]]. It throws a ball that spawns a [[Belle]].
## Spawning
Executive Sebastian has a 7.63% (1 in 13.1) chance to spawn instead of other [[Team Treasurite]] members in the [[Mithril Deposits]].
## Behavior
When encountered, Executive Sebastian will throw a ball that spawns a [[Belle]], a wolf with 6,500 [[Health]] that deals 450 [[Damage]]. Both Executive Sebastian and Belle will attempt to approach the player and attack. After a few seconds, the Belle will despawn, and Executive Sebastian can throw another ball to spawn another one.

## Trivia
- Executive Sebastian and Belle are most likely based off the French film, Belle and Sebastian, where a boy befriends a wild dog and names the dog, "Belle", or beautiful.
The ball thrown by Team Treasurite members to summon their associated creature
- The mobs are based on Team Rocket from Pokémon.
  - The ball that each mob throws in battle resembles Dive Ball in Pokémon.
  - The Grunt looks like a Team Rocket Grunt, Executive Sebastian looks like Proton, Executive Wendy looks like Ariana, and Executive Viper is most likely Archer. Corleone, as the leader, is probably the counterpart of Giovanni. Within Pokémon, Executives are the highest ranking members of Team Rocket after Giovanni.

# Executive Wendy
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Crystal Hollows
[Executive Wendy]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 62
Spawn Location: [[Mithril Deposits]]
Special Behavior: Summons Cavitak
Mob Type: [[Humanoid]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 18,000; [[Damage]]: 450
[Drops]
Drops: 8–15x Rough Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 8–15x Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 0–1x [[Treasurite]]
XP: +93 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 68
[Metadata]
ID: TEAM_TREASURITE_WENDY_100
Executive Wendy is a [[Mob]] found in the [[Mithril Deposits]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]] and a member of [[Team Treasurite]]. It throws a ball that spawns a [[Cavitak]].
## Spawning
Executive Wendy has a 7.63% (1 in 13.1) chance to spawn instead of other [[Team Treasurite]] members in the [[Mithril Deposits]].
## Behavior
When encountered, Executive Wendy will throw a ball that spawns a [[Cavitak]], a Cave Spider with 6,500 [[Health]] that deals 450 [[Damage]]. Cavitak and Executive Wendy will attempt to approach the player and attack them, with Cavitak despawning after a few seconds. Executive Wendy can summon another Cavitak by throwing a ball again.

## Trivia
The ball thrown by Team Treasurite members to summon their associated creature
- The mobs are based on Team Rocket from Pokémon.
  - The ball that each mob throws in battle resembles Dive Ball in Pokémon.
  - The Grunt looks like a Team Rocket Grunt, Executive Sebastian looks like Proton, Executive Wendy looks like Ariana, and Executive Viper is most likely Archer. Corleone, as the leader, is probably the counterpart of Giovanni. Within Pokémon, Executives are the highest ranking members of Team Rocket after Giovanni.

The Explosive Imp is a [[Mob]] that spawns during the [[Kuudra]] fight in [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
## Spawning
An Explosive Imp spawns when a [[Kuudra Berserker]] is killed.
## Behavior
Explosive Imps will run towards nearby players, damaging them and exploding on death.

[[Experience]] Orbs: 1
[Metadata]
ID: PEST_MOUSE_1
A Field Mouse is a [[Pests|Garden Pest]] that spawns in [[Pest Trap|Pest Traps]].
## Spawning
Field Mice can only spawn in [[Pest Trap|Pest Traps]], [[Mouse Trap|Mouse Traps]], or [[Vermin Trap|Vermin Traps]].
Unlike other pests, Field Mice have the same spawn rate regardless of what bait is used (except for [[Moondew]] which will always spawn a [[Lunar Moth]]).
The spawn rate of Field Mice changes based on what type of trap is used.
|Pest Trap|Spawn Rate|
|-|-|
|[[Pest Trap]]|2.78% (1 in 36)|
|[[Mouse Trap]]|7.89% (1 in 12.67)|
|[[Vermin Trap]]|10.26% (1 in 9.75)|
The chance for a Field Mouse is also affected by [[Tracking]].
## Behavior
Field Mice run away from the player. Field Mice can only be damaged with [[Vacuums]] or [[Lassos]].

|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|310|310|310|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|227|227|227|
Killing a Fire Mage will make the player gain [[Factions|5 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]] and lose [[Factions|5 ቾ Mage Reputation]], up to a limit of [[Factions|0 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]] and [[Factions|0 ቾ Mage Reputation]].
## Behavior
Fire Mages only attack the player when provoked, or if they have less than [[Factions|-1000 ቾ Mage Reputation]]. They have two attacks:
- Shoot a beam of fire for 8 seconds, which deals large amounts of damage.
- Shoot a barrage of fire for 2 seconds, which deals large amounts of damage, but has a 3 second cooldown.
When neutral they hold nothing, but when provoked, they appear to hold [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]].

## Spawning
Fireflies can spawn in the [[The Garden]], starting at [[The Garden|Garden XI (11)]]. Fireflies can only spawn during the night.
## Behavior
Fireflies fly away from the player. Fireflies can only be damaged with [[Vacuums]] or [[Lassos]].

# Flame Dragon
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Flame Dragon]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 765
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]
Mob Type: [[Arcane]]; [[Ender]]; [[Airborne]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000,000,000; [[Defense]]: 2,500; [[Damage]]: 720,000
[Drops]
XP: +1,147 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 841
[Metadata]
ID: MASTER_WITHER_KING_DRAGON_100
The Flame Dragon, also titled Withered Dragon, is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the final phase of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|The Master Mode Catacombs - Floor VII]] boss fight.
## Boss Fight
The bottom of Master Mode Floor VII contains a giant throne room. There are five Relics placed next to Dragon statues around the room. There are also five cauldrons in front of the throne near the center of the room. They each have a specifically colored Clay below them. The players must bring and place the Relics with the same corresponding color as the Cauldron's block of clay. By doing so, The Wither King will be summoned on his throne.
Damage cannot be dealt towards [[The Wither King]], and he mostly acts as a spectator to the battle. The Wither King spawns five dragons with special abilities, including the Flame Dragon. All five dragons must be killed near their corresponding colored Dragon statue to destroy them. All Dragons can respawn at any time indefinitely until all dragon statues are destroyed. If all five statues are destroyed, the floor is completed.
## Behavior
The Flame Dragon will shoot fireballs at players and will occasionally fly close to the ground, setting it on fire. It leaves a trail of fire in its wake.

# Flare
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Crimson Isle
[Flare]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 240

[Metadata]
ID: FLARE_90
Flare is a [[Blaze]] type [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Magma Chamber]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
Flares spawn on 12 different stone pillars which surround the [[Magma Chamber]] platform. They all respawn in intervals of around 10 seconds and only one can spawn on each pillar at a time.
## Behavior
Flares are three [[Blaze]] mobs stacked on top of each other with each blaze sharing the same [[Health]] pool of 5,000,000 with each other. They act as sentries shooting high damage fireballs. However, they do not take knockback, move, or face the player making them easy targets from a distance. Their range of attack is also considered poor for a sentry-like mob, typically anyone standing around the Magma Chamber (for example while fighting the [[Magma Boss]]) will never be targeted by them. Anyone approaching the area through the stairs may get targeted, though.
If a flare misses its attack, it will not be able to throw another fireball for a while, rendering it vulnerable for follow-up attacks by the player as they have no melee attacks. Caution should be taken when flares are respawning as they attack immediately on sight.

## Trivia
- A zombie can sometimes be seen which the blazes are riding on. These zombies are normally supposed to be invisible and possibly responsible for keeping the blazes in place and not float up.
- The soul of Flares can be captured using [[Necromancy]] items, which is odd as normally souls of blaze mobs are not able to be absorbed. However, it will neither spawn when used or even use any mana despite showing its stats when inspecting it. It is likely that it is trying to summon the aforementioned invisible zombie which has no stats, which would also explain why it was able to drop a soul in the first place.
- Due to them being three mobs stacked on top of each other, AOE damage can potentially kill flares in one hit.
- Despite being a Blaze-type mob, killing them does not contribute to spawning the [[Inferno Demonlord]] slayer boss, or increasing the counter on the [[Vanquished Blaze Belt|Vanquished Blaze Belt.]]
- Flares are considered one of the best mobs when grinding for [[Vanquisher|Vanquishers]], as Flares spawn at fixed locations and don't move.
- Similarly, Flares are considered the best mobs to gain a top score in [[Spooky Festival]], although they favor dropping more Green Candies, in which case [[Ghost|Ghosts]] are ideal to gain Purple Candies instead.
## Bugs
- If a [[Gauntlet of Contagion]] is used, killing a Flare will count as three kills.

A Flint Skeleton is a [[Mob]] that spawns on the [[Spider's Den]].
## Spawning
Flint Skeletons spawn on the [[Spider's Den]], near the [[Launch Pad (Structure)|Launch Pad]] to [[The End]] and above the [[Arachne's Burrow]].
## Behavior
A Flint Skeleton fires arrows at nearby players, dealing [[Damage]] on hit. When killed, Flint Skeletons drop a [[Bone]] on the ground, which is not affected by [[Auto-pickup]]. If the [[Bone]] is not picked up by the player for 5 seconds, another Flint Skeleton will immediately respawn in the Bone's location.

# Ghast (Minion Mob)

ID: GENERATOR_GHAST_1
The Ghast is a [[Mob]] that can be summoned from [[Ghast Minion|Ghast Minions]].
## Spawning
Ghasts can spawn from [[Ghast Minion|Ghast Minions]] on the player's [[Private Island]].
## Behavior
Ghasts will fly around and frequently shoot fireballs at nearby players. The shooting of fireballs can be prevented with the [[Intimidation Talisman]] or greater.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv8]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ghast Tear]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv8]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|2|2|2|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|12|12|12|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|8|8|8|
## Bugs
- Ghasts spawned with a non-air block in their hitbox are not be able to move. They can rotate to aim at the player.
  - Ghasts can spawn with a non-air block in their hitbox as the minion only checks a 3x3x3 area for air, while the Ghast's hitbox is larger.
## Trivia
- At one point, killing Ghasts spawned by [[Ghast Minion|Ghast Minions]] were the best method to earn [[Combat|Combat XP]]. It has been changed so that mobs on the [[Private Island]] don't give [[Combat|Combat XP]].

# Ghost
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Dwarven Mines
[Ghost]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 157
Spawn Location: [[The Mist]]
Mob Type: [[Arcane]] [[Subterranean]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000,000; [[Damage]]: 1,000; [[True Damage]]: 100
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1,000,000 [[Coins]] 0–1x [[Sorrow]] 0–1x [[Plasma]] 0–1x [[Ghostly Boots]] 0–1x [[Volta]]
XP: +100 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 172
[Metadata]
ID: CAVERNS_GHOST_250
Ghosts are invisible Charged Creepers found in [[The Mist]].
## Spawning
Ghosts spawn in large numbers throughout [[The Mist]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Behavior
Ghosts are hostile to any nearby players. They have 1M [[Health]] and move slowly. They have a periodic AoE attack of a 4 block radius that deals more damage the closer they are to players. They deal 1000 [[Damage]] along with 100 [[True Damage]]; if the player gets within 1 block of them, the damage is doubled. They also do not take knockback.
Ghosts can take damage only from melee attacks. Most magic abilities, bows (including [[Bonemerang|Bonemerangs]]), and damage over time effects (like the [[Phoenix Pet]]'s Fourth Flare and the [[Wither Skeleton Pet]]'s Death Touch) do not affect Ghosts. This does not include enchantments such as [[Fire Aspect]] and [[Venomous]]. Additionally, this restriction does not apply on the [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] game mode.
Certain equipment, such as [[Flaming Fist]] or [[Gauntlet of Contagion]] can damage ghosts despite not being melee attacks.
Their name tags and health bars do not show. This includes any indication of Runic ghosts, making their only giveaway being increased [[Health]] and [[Runecrafting]] experience upon death.
## Drops

|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Volta]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.2%|

|[[Ghostly Boots]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.01%|
|[[Coins]]|1000000|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.01%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv157]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|52|52|52|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|100|100|100|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|172|172|172|
Ghost can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Ghost Shard]] and +250 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+2,500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

## Tips
- Certain [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]], such as the [[Duplico Shard]] and [[Driftling Shard]], boost [[Damage]] against Ghosts by up to a total of 60%.
  - Other [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]], such as the [[Hideonwall Shard]] and [[Chuckwalla Shard]], grant 0.5% [[Magic Find]] per level up to 10 [[Magic Find]].
  - The [[Brineling Shard]] and [[Shyworm Shard]] boost +4 [[Defense]] per level up to +80 [[Defense]].
- The Lonesome Miner [[Heart of the Mountain]] perk and the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] or higher [[Mithril Golem Pet]]'s Subterranean Battler ability significantly increase damage dealt to mobs on mining islands, including Ghosts.
- The ability of [[Crimson Armor]] can be used to reach 1 million damage easier.
- Using a [[Medium Pocket Black Hole]] and a [[Prime Huntaxe - Nex Titanum|Huntaxe]] is currently the optimal way of gaining [[Hunting]] XP outside of fusions, though survivability can prove problematic without healing from enchantments such as [[Drain]] or [[Vampirism]].
## Trivia
- The first time the player kills a Ghost, they will receive the [[Achievements|"Ghost Buster" Achievement]].
- If the Ghost drops 1,000,000 [[Coins]], the following message will appear in chat: The ghost's death materialized 1,000,000 coins from the mists!
- Ghosts used to drop [[Bag of Cash]] which could have been sold to NPCs for 1,000,000 [[Coins]] and, unlike the cash itself they drop now, was affected by [[Magic Find]]
- Ghosts was the first mob to implement loot sharing feature.
- Ghosts were often used as a method of killing off AFK Goblin or Ice Walker grinders as well as trolling players by leaving a Ghost at the Dwarven Mines spawn point, killing players as they joined the lobby, many times causing huge losses of coins and potion effects. This was patched on April 25, 2022, when Ghosts were made to despawn past a certain Y coordinate, making it impossible to fish them out of the Mist.
  - Currently, fishing rods do not have the ability to even lift ghosts up from the ground, with the only way of elevating them now being via the usage of a [[Black Holes|Pocket Black Hole]] on a low health ghost.
- Ghosts recover 10% of player's [[Health]] when killed, making them the only mob to recover [[Health]] on defeat without an ability causing them to do so.

# Glacite Maniacs
Aliases: Glacite Maniac, Maniacs
Categories: Mobs, Dwarven Mines
The Glacite Maniacs are a group of [[Glacial (Mob Type)|Glacial]] mobs that spawn in [[Glacite Mineshafts]]. They have high defense, but take more damage based on the [[Breaking Power]] of the tool used to attack them.
## Mechanics
All Glacite Maniacs spawn when [[Glacite Mineshafts]] are generated, and unlike most other mobs, they do not respawn after dying. A commission titled Maniac Slayer requires killing any 10 of them to be completed. Unlike some other mob groups, each Glacite Maniac has its own Bestiary.

The Glacite Mutt is a [[Mob]] that can be found in the [[Glacite Mineshafts]]. They have high defense, but take more damage based on the [[Breaking Power]] of the tool used to attack them.
## Spawning
The Glacite Mutts spawn after [[Glacite Mineshafts]] are generated.
## Behavior
The Glacite Mutt will run towards the player and attack them with melee attacks from a short distance, dealing damage and -1 [[Cold]].

# Glacite Walker
Aliases: Ice Walker, Ice Walkers
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Dwarven Mines
[Glacite Walker]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 24
Spawn Location: [[Great Ice Wall]]
Mob Type: Glacial ([[Glacial (Mob Type)]]) [[Humanoid]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 888; [[Defense]]: 1000; [[Damage]]: 300
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x Glacite Armor Piece ([[Glacite Armor]]) 0–1x [[Glacite Jewel]]
XP: +36 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 26
[Metadata]
ID: ICE_WALKER_45
The Glacite Walker is a hostile mob that spawns in groups at the [[Great Ice Wall]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. They have high defense, but take more damage based on the [[Breaking Power]] of the tool used to attack them.
## Behavior
- 16 [[Glacite Walker]] Glacite Walkers spawn every 11 seconds at the [[Great Ice Wall]].
- They are hostile to all players. The point at which they stop pursuit varies with each Glacite Walker, and occasionally wander then fall into [[The Mist]].
- As [[Glacial (Mob Type)|Glacial]] mobs, they are immune to all damage except coming from:
  - [[Huntaxes]];
  - items with a non-zero [[Breaking Power]] stat.
|Location|Spawn Spots (The number of spots in the location where this mob can spawn in.)|Spawn Delay (The time, after which a new mob spawns in every spot.)|Max per spot (The maximum number of mobs that can exist in each spot at a time.)|
|-|-|-|-|
|Glacite Walker|16|11 seconds|1|
### Commissions
The Glacite Walker Slayer commission requires the player to kill 50 [[Glacite Walker]] Glacite Walkers.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv24]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Glacite Jewel]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.5%|

[Non-item drops - [Lv24]]

|[[Coins]]|8|8|8|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|36|36|36|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|26|26|26|
Glacite Walker can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Glacite Walker Shard]] and +150 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+1,500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

## Trivia
- Much like [[Goblin (disambiguation)|Goblins]] and [[Treasure Hoarder|Treasure Hoarders]], Glacite Walkers are unstable, meaning if they are killed the first few frames upon spawning, in which they are labeled as Armor Stands, it may result in a game crash. This is due to the spawning of this mob will create a Team that is already existing and is a fatal error to the game program.
- The damage dealt to [[Glacial (Mob Type)|Glacial]] mobs is also determined by [[Breaking Power]] as every 1 is 150x more potent than 1 [[Damage]].
  - The maximum possible damage multiplier is 1800x at 12 [[Breaking Power]] , using a [[Reforging|Scraped]] [[Divan's Drill]] and the Maniac Miner pickaxe ability.
  - The damage dealt can be further increased using the [[Titan Killer]] enchantment, which can be applied to a [[Gemstone Gauntlet]].
## Tips
- Glacite Walkers are a great source of early game [[Combat|Combat XP]].
- The [[Stonk]] is little effective against them because of its low [[Breaking Power]] and lack of a [[Damage]] stat. A [[Wooden Pickaxe]] would deal 4x [[Damage]] the damage of the former.

The Globowl is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Colosseum (Rift)|Colosseum]].
## Behavior
Globowl spawn during the [[Bacte]] boss fight. They will jump towards the player. To start the Bacte fight, the player must hit Globowls into the original slime. The player can also hit Globowls into [[Blobbercyst|Blobbercysts]] to deal 100 [[Rift Damage]] to them, or deal 1 [[Damage]] to [[Bacte]]'s tentacles.

Goblins spawn throughout the [[Goblin Burrows]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].

## Spawning
Goblin Flamethrowers spawn in the [[Goblin Holdout]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]] in certain structures.
## Behavior
Goblin Flamethrowers shoot fire at players as a ranged attack.

# Golden Goblin
Aliases: Gold Goblin, Gold goblin
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Confirmations needed, Dwarven Mines
[Golden Goblin]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 99
Spawn Location: [[Goblin Egg]] [[Dwarven Mines]] [[Crystal Hollows]]
Mob Type: [[Timid]] [[Shielded]] [[Elusive]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 3 Hits; [[Damage]]: 250 (Necromancy)
[Drops]
Drops: 5x [[Mithril]] 2–3x [[Gold Essence]]
XP: +148 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 108
[Metadata]
ID: GOBLIN_50
Golden Goblin is a passive [[Mob]] that spawns from [[Goblin Egg|Goblin Eggs]], or by mining in the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Spawning
Golden Goblins are a rare spawn from throwing [[Goblin Egg|Goblin Eggs]] in [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Glacite Tunnels]] or [[Glacite Mineshafts]]:
|Item|Chance to Spawn|
|-|-|
|[[Goblin Egg]]|2% (1 in 50 eggs)|
|[[Green Goblin Egg]]|2.5% (1 in 40 eggs)|
|[[Yellow Goblin Egg]]|3.33% (1 in 30 eggs)|
|[[Red Goblin Egg]]|5% (1 in 20 eggs)|
|[[Blue Goblin Egg]]|10% (1 in 10 eggs)|
Chance to spawn can be increased with [[Heart of the Mountain|Sky Mall Perk]], [[Heart of the Mountain|Luck of the Cave Perk]], [[Heart of the Mountain|Tunnel Vision Ability]] and [[Attributes|Dwarven Serendipity]] Attribute.
Golden Goblins also have a chance to spawn when mining certain blocks in certain areas:
|Location|Block|Chance to Spawn|
|-|-|-|
|[[Dwarven Mines]]/[[Glacite Tunnels]]|[[Dwarven Metals]], Pure Ores ([[Ores]]), Gemstones ([[Gemstone]])|0.1% (1 in 1,000 ores)|
|[[Crystal Hollows]]|Pure Gold ([[Gold Ingot]]), Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]), [[Mithril]]|0.1% (1 in 1,000 ores)|
|[[Crystal Hollows]]|[[Hard Stone]]|0.01% (1 in 10,000 ores)|
Chance to spawn can be increased with [[Heart of the Mountain|Sky Mall Perk]], [[Heart of the Mountain|Luck of the Cave Perk]], [[Heart of the Mountain|Tunnel Vision Ability]], [[Attributes|Dwarven Serendipity]] Attribute and [[Reforging|Ambered]] reforge.
When the Golden Goblin spawns, the following message will display in chat: A Golden Goblin has spawned! Golden Goblins will de-spawn after 30 seconds.
Golden Goblins have a 3.33% chance to be replaced by [[Diamond Goblin|Diamond Goblins]]. This chance can be increased by [[Gemma|Return to Sender Perk]] and [[Tracking]].
## Behavior
Golden Goblins run away from the player, and jump off cliffs. Their behavior is similar to the Hidden Jerrys that can be spawned during [[Candidate Jerry|Mayor Jerry]]'s time in office.

|[[Gold Essence]]|+1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|30%|

## Trivia
- The first time spawning a Golden Goblin from throwing a [[Goblin Egg]] will grant the [[Achievements|"The Next Generation" Achievement]].
- [[Shadow Fury]] can be used to chase a Golden Goblin even if out of sight.
- Killing 1 Golden Goblin is a possible task for Commission Slot 2 (or 4, with Peak of the Mountain level 3).[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
  - Using [[Mineral Armor]] with a [[Stonk]] in the [[Puzzler]]'s room used to be a way to complete this quest quickly. However, this was patched when the ores in the Puzzler's room were filled with planks.
- Golden Goblins contribute to the 100 necessary kills in the Goblin Slayer commission.
- Killing Golden Goblins contributes to the tiered [[Achievements|"Goblin Killer" Achievement]].
- Despite being an [[Elusive]] mob, chance to spawn a Golden Goblin is not increased by [[Tracking]].
