# Goldor
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Pages with UIs, More info needed, Catacombs, Dungeons, Bosses, Dungeon Mobs
[Goldor]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: BOSS ([[Bosses]])
Mob Level: 717-1,166
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]
Description: The Wither Lords; Disciples of the Wither King. Inherited the Catacombs eons ago. Never defeated, feared by anything living AND dead.
Mob Type: [[Wither]] [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 750,000,000-1,200,000,000; [[Defense]]: 1,800 (Master Mode); [[Damage]]: 7,200-720,000
[Metadata]
ID: GOLDOR_1; MASTER_GOLDOR_1
Goldor is a [[Bosses|BOSS]] along with [[Maxor]], [[Storm]] and [[Necron]] of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] and is a disciple of [[The Wither King]].
## Collection
Killing Goldor and ALL the other Withers increases the Necron collection.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Wither Blood]] Reward|+15|
|II|100|Golden Necron Head ([[Golden Heads]]) Reward; Gold ([[Gold Essence]]) Essence|+15|
|III|150|Wither Helmet ([[Wither Armor]]) Reward|+15|
|IV|250|[[Recombobulator 3000]] Reward|+25|
|V|500|Wither Leggings ([[Wither Armor]]) Reward|+25|
|VI|750|Wither Chestplate ([[Wither Armor]]) Reward|+25|
|VII|1,000|Diamond Necron Head ([[Diamond Heads]]) Recipe; Diamond ([[Diamond Essence]]) Essence|+25|
## Boss Fight
The boss fight is split into four phases. (Five phases in Master Mode.) There are four Wither Lords: Maxor, Storm, Goldor and Necron. Each Wither will be a boss for a phase that must be killed individually for each phase.
In all parts of the boss fight, lava damages the player for 20% of their max health, as well as launching them upwards in one of 2 ways:
- Regular: You are launched into the air at a standard height ([More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) blocks).
- Super: Looking upwards at a pitch of -40 or lower triggers a Super lava bounce, boosting you 30% higher than a Regular jump.
### Phase 3
Storm in Phase 2 will destroy the broken red crusher with a beam, breaking the red pad below and revealing the third layer and the next boss: Goldor. Players must jump down into the third layer; the beam can target players, dealing damage and knockback. After a short while, Goldor will follow the players in at a slow and steady pace while throwing his Greatswords.
The third layer comprises four sections with three gates between them. Within each section, there are four [[Terminal|Terminals]], two levers and one device, which must all be completed, except the second section, which has five terminals. These terminals are located on the walls, with lava on the floor and floating platforms connecting them. There are also some TNT traps hanging on top of each section. They will detonate if hit by Goldor's Greatsword. After everything is completed within one section, there is a five-second delay before the gate opens, and players can proceed to the next section. Alternatively, if the player destroys the gate using either [[Superboom TNT]] or Guided Sheep, it skips the delay, and the gate opens once everything is done within one section.
In this section, Goldor cannot be harmed. His frenzy now deals extremely high damage. After Goldor passes a section, it starts to destroy itself with explosions that harm any remaining players. Dealing damage to Goldor slows him down. The [[Spider Pet]]'s Web-Weaver perk (Rare+) can slow down Goldor further, allowing for more time to complete the terminals.
After completing all Terminals, Goldor becomes surrounded by swords to block player's attacks and launch Wither Skulls at a fast rate. In exchange, Goldor is vulnerable to any form of attack, though caution is advised as his frenzy is still active. Once Goldor dies, the players will progress into the next phase.

|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|When Phase 3 starts|[BOSS] Goldor: Don't think you can hide from me <player> [BOSS] Goldor: Who dares trespass into my domain? [BOSS] Goldor: Little ants, plotting and scheming, thinking they are invincible… [BOSS] Goldor: I won’t let you break the factory core, I gave my life to my Master. [BOSS] Goldor: No one matches me in close quarters.|
|When gate is destroyed|[BOSS] Goldor: Punishment served, nothing survives my reach.|
|When players go in core|[BOSS] Goldor: You have done it, you destroyed the factory… [BOSS] Goldor: But you have nowhere to hide anymore! [BOSS] Goldor: YOU ARE FACE TO FACE WITH GOLDOR!|
|When killed|[BOSS] Goldor: ... [BOSS] Goldor: Necron, forgive me.|
|If all players die|[BOSS] Goldor: FINALLY! This took way too long. [BOSS] Goldor: Now that you're a Ghost, can you help me clean up?|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None|[BOSS] Goldor: Closer to me!|
|None|[BOSS] Goldor: CLOSER!|
|None|[BOSS] Goldor: Come Closer!|
|None|[BOSS] Goldor: Do you really think we won’t repair everything? Your impact will be minuscule!|

|When hit in terminal sections|[BOSS] Goldor: There is no stopping me down there!|
## Trivia
- Goldor was going to be the [[Bosses|BOSS]] of [[The Catacombs - Floor VIII]] before Hypixel removed [[Maxor]], Goldor and [[Storm]] from the game and replaced them with [[Necron]].
  - All three bosses were later re-added to The Catacombs - Floor VII.
- [[Goldor's Armor]] and [[Goldor the Fish]] are references to this boss.
- Goldor could be slowed by using a [[Phantom Rod]].
- Goldor is the only Wither Lord that never gets stunned during its fighting phase. Maxor, Storm and Necron all have their respective states during which they are stunned and able to take damage.
- Goldor is the only Wither Lord to use a normal texture of his base mob, the Wither. Maxor, Storm and Necron all use the blue texture of that particular mob.

The Goliath Barbarian is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Barbarian Outpost]]. It is a stronger version of the [[Barbarian]].
## Spawning
The Goliath Barbarian can rarely spawn in the [[Barbarian Outpost]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Drops

Killing a Goliath Barbarian when the player is in the [[Factions|Barbarian Faction]] will make them lose [[Factions|5 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]]. If the player is in the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] and they kill a Goliath Barbarian, they will gain [[Factions|25 ቾ Mage Reputation]], only if they have negative reputation.
## Behavior
When attacked, the Goliath Barbarian will run towards the player and try to attack them, dealing 4,000 [[Damage]] on hit. Every 10 seconds, the Goliath Barbarian will gain more maximum [[Health]], and set its health to that number. Its maximum [[Health]] is capped at 11M .

# Grizzly Bear
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Grizzly Bear]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 101
Spawn Location: [[Torrhus Canyon]]
Mob Type: [[Woodland]] [[Animal]] [[Elusive]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 200000; [[Damage]]: 400
[Drops]
Drops: 8–16x [[Enchanted Helix Log]] 1x [[Grizzly Bear Shard]] 0–1x [[Helixis]]
XP: +151 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 111
[Metadata]
ID: GRIZZLY_BEAR_101
A Grizzly Bear is a [[Mob]] on the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Spawning
A Grizzly Bear has a 1% chance to spawn when breaking a tree in the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
## Behavior
Grizzly Bears attempt to charge at the Player trying to lasso it, breaking free from the lasso if they damage the Player with the message: The Grizzly Bear broke free from your lasso!
After the player depletes the Grizzly Bear's stamina, the Grizzly Bear will break free from the lasso with the message: The Grizzly Bear destroyed your Lasso! Guess you'll have to fight it!
The Player must then damage the Grizzly Bear with an axe until it is almost defeated, then finish hunting it with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]]. The Grizzly Bear cannot be killed by an axe and will remain at 1 [[Health]]. However, it takes fall damage and can thus die without rewarding shards, such as by being knocked off of a platform or falling from one during a charge.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv101]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Helix Log]]|8-16|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Grizzly Bear Shard]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Helixis]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv101]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|33|33|33|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|151|151|151|
|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|1000|1000|1000|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|111|111|111|
Catching a Grizzly Bear with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] and [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] grants [[Grizzly Bear Shard]] and +1,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Grizzly Bear can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] to obtain [[Grizzly Bear Shard]] and +10,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. Since this mob is [[Elusive]], [[Charm Chance|Charming]] is guaranteed to proc on kill.

## Tips
- The Grizzly Bear charges when each bar of its health is depleted, at about the same time as the game says "REEL".
  - A well-timed jump can thus cause the Grizzly Bear to miss its charge and remain on the lasso.
- It is recommended to get to some sort of high ground inaccessible to the Grizzly Bear in order to lasso it, if not being aided by other players.
  - When this is done, the Grizzly Bear may erratically charge side to side, so the player should prepare to follow its movement well enough to avoid the lasso breaking.
